        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Hydroxyzine HCl",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "First Generation Antihistamines"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Treatment for anxiety. Management of pruritus and urticaria. Adjunct to pre- and post-op medication; to opioid analgesia in the management of cancer pain."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Tab - Adults - Short-term management of anxiety:</b> 50-100 mg 4x daily; <b>pre- or post-op sedation:</b> 50-100 mg. <b>Pruritus - Adults:</b> Initially 25 mg at night, may increase to 25 mg 3-4x daily; <b>Childn >6 yrs:</b> Initially 15-25 mg daily, may increase to 50 mg daily in divided doses or max 2 mg/kg; <b>Childn 1-6 yrs:</b> 1 mg/kg, may be given in divided doses, max 2.5 mg/kg. <b>IM inj - Psychiatric and emotional emergencies (e.g. acute alcoholism) - Adults:</b> 100 mg immediately and every 4-6 hrs as needed. <b>Nausea and vomiting (excluding pregnant) - Adults:</b> 25-200 mg; <b>Childn:</b> 0.5 mg/lb BW. <b>Adjunct to pre- and post-op - Adults:</b> 25-200 mg; <b>Childn:</b> 0.5 mg/lb BW; <b>Pre and postpartum adjunctive therapy - Childn:</b> 25-100 mg."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Mark signs of damage caused by ototoxic drugs (e.g. aminoglycoside antibiotics). May suppress cutaneous histamine response to allergen extracts. Discontinue several days before skin testing.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "CNS depression, lassitude, dizziness, and incoordination. Headache, psychomotor impairment, antimuscarinic effects (e.g. dry mouth, thickened respiratory tract secretions, blurred vision, urinary difficulty or retention, constipation, increased gastric reflux)."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "CNS depressants (e.g. alcohol, barbiturates, hypnotics, opioid analgesics, anxiolytic sedatives, antipsychotics). Antimuscarinics (e.g. atropine, TCAs, MAOIs). Astemizole."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Tablet 25 mg (P23.00). Solution for injection 50 mg/mL (P245.00)."
          }
        ],
        "company": {
          "id": 1609,

  {
    "id": 4568,
    "detail": {
      "success": true,
      "data": {
        "id": 4568,
        "brand_name": "Zincretin",
        "brand_suffix": null,
        "brand_shot": {
          "original": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/ddb9So4w53ePpBUt3nc28OkZ6pEo2ZyJauHlbyJ7w5uZI3TjTr.jpg",
          "medium": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/ltXtZX4YTn4u8Y4liWDPeki8xX3B9vUvKYjlDVNQhlI8Hggg90.jpg",
          "small": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/JvZeF4ueJ34PSV0TMvQ3uai1cnpBOnfJwKv6c00IS5eVtt6U1L.jpg"
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Sitagliptin phosphate",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Dipeptidyl Peptidase-4 (DPP-4) Inhibitors"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "For improvement of glycemic control in adults with type 2 diabetes mellitus: (1) as monotherapy in patients inadequately controlled by diet and exercise alone and for whom metformin is inappropriate due to contraindications or intolerance; (2) as dual therapy with metformin, a sulfonylurea, or a PPAR-gamma agonist when diet and exercise plus the individual agent alone do not provide adequate glycemic control; (3) as triple therapy in combination with metformin and a sulfonylurea or a PPAR-gamma agonist when diet and exercise plus dual therapy with these agents do not provide adequate glycemic control; (4) as add-on to insulin (with or without metformin) when diet and exercise plus stable dose of insulin do not provide adequate glycemic control."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "100 mg once daily. <b>In combination with metformin and/or PPAR-gamma agonist</b>: Maintain dose and administer sitagliptin concomitantly. <b>In combination with sulfonylurea or insulin</b>: Consider lower dose for sulfonylurea or insulin. <b>Renal impairment - Moderate (CrCl &ge;30-&lt;50 mL/min)</b>: 50 mg once daily. <b>Severe (CrCl &lt;30 mL/min) or with ESRD (requiring hemodialysis or peritoneal dialysis)</b>: 25 mg once daily."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity. Pregnancy and lactation."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Not to be used for type 1 DM or treatment of diabetic ketoacidosis. Patients with history of pancreatitis, renal impairment, ESRD requiring hemodialysis or peritoneal dialysis. May induce hypoglycemia when used with insulin or sulfonylurea. Discontinue if bullous pemphigoid is suspected. Childn and adolescents &lt;18 yrs.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Hypoglycemia, headache."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "Potent CYP3A4 inhibitors (i.e. ketoconazole, itraconazole, ritonavir, clarithromycin), digoxin."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Film-coated tablet 50 mg (P30.00), 100 mg (P30.00)."
          }
        ],
        "company": {
          "id": 1583,
          "name": "Zydus Healthcare Philippines Inc."
        },

  {
    "id": 4920,
    "detail": {
      "success": true,
      "data": {
        "id": 4920,
        "brand_name": "Zinnat 500",
        "brand_suffix": null,
        "brand_shot": {
          "original": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/Xg48XXTBwiSFSVuNp9tCmtqliI1EUSPfxwuB40dyDdJNAFq1rE.jpg",
          "medium": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/fxHaVRYgFVTWXbVRzYdTdRy0DtHsWX7CaibP3gPAZvbzJoy5Fv.jpg",
          "small": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/mudJajqn3gOSd8VkLocB3S0YsgyGOq7SfcoVAjc8T146nMshr3.jpg"
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Cefuroxime axetil",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Second Generation Cephalosporins"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Treatment of upper respiratory tract (e.g. ear, nose, and throat infections such as otitis media, sinusitis, tonsillitis, pharyngitis), lower respiratory tract (e.g. pneumonia, acute exacerbations of chronic bronchitis), genito-urinary tract (pyelonephritis, cystitis, urethritis), skin, and soft tissue infections (furunculosis, pyoderma, impetigo), gonorrhea, acute uncomplicated gonococcal urethritis, and cervicitis; early Lyme disease and subsequent prevention of late Lyme disease."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Treatment duration:</b> 7 days (range: 5-10 days). <b>Adults - Most infections,  UTIs, mild to moderate LRTIs, pyelonephritis:</b> 250 mg 2x daily; <b>More severe LRTIs or if pneumonia is suspected:</b> 500 mg 2x daily; <b>Lyme disease in adults and childn >12 yrs:</b> 500 mg 2x daily for 14 days (range: 10-21 days); <b>Uncomplicated gonorrhea:</b> 1 g once daily. <b>Sequential therapy - Pneumonia:</b> 1.5 g IV/IM cefuroxime sodium 2x or 3x daily for 48-72 hrs, followed by 500 mg oral cefuroxime (as axetil) 2x daily for 7-10 days; <b>Acute exacerbations of chronic bronchitis:</b> 750 mg IV/IM cefuroxime sodium 2x or 3x daily for 48-72 hrs, followed by 500 mg oral cefuroxime (as axetil) 2x daily for 5-10 days. <b>Childn - Most infections:</b> 125 mg 2x daily; <b>Otitis media or where appropriate, with more severe infection:</b> 250 mg 2x daily; <b>Lyme disease in childn < 12 yrs:</b> 250 mg 2x daily for 14 days (range: 10-21 days). <b>Renal impairment:</b> 125-500 mg; <b>CrCl 10-29 mL/min:</b> Every 24 hrs; <b>< 10 mL/min:</b> Every 48 hrs; <b>During hemodialysis:</b> Single additional dose given at end of each dialysis."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Overgrowth of <i>Candida</i> or non-susceptible organisms; pseudomembranous colitis; Jarisch-Herxheimer reaction. Immediately discontinue treatment if prolonged or significant diarrhea or abdominal cramps occurs.May impair ability to drive or operate machinery. Childn < 3 months. Pregnancy and lactation.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Overgrowth of <i>Candida</i>, eosinophilia, headache, dizziness, diarrhea, nausea, abdominal pain, transient increase in hepatic enzyme levels."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "Oral contraceptives. Ferricyanide test."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Film-coated tablet 500 mg (P82.00)."
          }
        ],
        "company": {
          "id": 601,
          "name": "STADA Philippines, Inc."
        },
        "class": "human",
        "form_class": "3",
        "prescription_class": "Rx",
        "description": null,

        "instrument": null,

        "content": "Fenofibrate",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Fibric Acid Derivatives & Related Agents"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Adjunctive therapy to diet for the treatment of hypertriglyceridemia (Fredrickson types IV and V) and  primary hypercholesterolemia or mixed dyslipidemia (Fredrickson types IIa, IIb) who are at risk of pancreatitis and who do not respond adequately to dietary effort."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Adults:</b> Initially 200-400 mg daily in divided doses with food."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hepatic and renal insufficiency. Concomitant use with other fibrates or HMG-CoA reductase inhibitors. Childn."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Monitor liver function regularly. Discontinue if enzyme levels persist above 3x the normal limit. Pregnancy and lactation.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Headache, vertigo, digestive, abdominal pain, nausea, vomiting, diarrhea, flatulence, rashes, pruritus, urticarial, photosensitivity reactions, fatigue."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "Other fibrates, oral anticoagulant, ciclosporin."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Capsule 200 mg (P24.50)."
          }
        ],

  {
    "id": 3105,
    "detail": {
      "success": true,
      "data": {
        "id": 3105,
        "brand_name": "Ziproc",
        "brand_suffix": null,
        "brand_shot": {
          "original": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/jIRL82SMm8VTtRHFsR7GcbHbiuSfoiEBism7AbxU9RJIK7S1eh.jpg",
          "medium": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/Mguco7lxHlbW9wDTw9AsAG2upxgVVDiA1WCPO9bmx1m0gqaa2X.jpg",
          "small": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/OE4ejYYgqd0DuumE2YhKRn8ogeMb6rWoCjef4AAnBL6bJ5LGtO.jpg"
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Clozapine",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Atypical Antipsychotics"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Management of schizophrenia; reserved to patients who failed to respond to, or who experience severe extrapyramidal side effects with classical antipsychotics. Reduce the risk of recurrent suicidal behavior in those with schizophrenia or schizoaffective disorder who is at risk of suicidal behavior. Management of treatment-resistant psychoses associated with Parkinson’s disease."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Schizophrenia including reducing the risk of suicidal behavior – Usual dose</b>: 12.5 mg once or twice daily on the 1st day followed by 25 mg twice daily on the 2<sup>nd</sup> day, then gradually increase to 25-50 mg to achieve 300 mg within 2-3 weeks. Subsequent increments of 50-100 mg may be made once or twice weekly; <b>Therapeutic dose</b>: 200-450 mg daily in divided doses; Max: 900 mg/day in divided doses; Maintenance: &le;200 mg single dose in the evening; <b>Ending therapy</b>: Withdraw gradually over 1-2 weeks period. <b>Psychoses in Parkinson’s disease – Initial dose</b>: Not &lt;12.5 mg once daily in the evening, subsequent increase to 12.5 mg, up to 2 increases each week, 50 mg should not be reached before the end of 2<sup>nd</sup> week; <b>Therapeutic dose</b>: 25-37.5 mg daily, may be increased to 50 mg at weekly intervals; Max: 100 mg daily; Maintenance: 12.5 mg, max 100 mg daily, as single dose or 2 divided doses; <b>Ending therapy</b>: Withdraw in decrements of 12.5 mg over 1-2 weeks."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Patients with uncontrolled epilepsy, alcoholic or toxic psychoses, drug intoxication, circulatory collapse, bone marrow suppression, myeloproliferative disorders, abnormalities of WBC, neutropenia or agranulocytosis (except that due to chemotherapy); severe renal impairment, history of cardiac impairment; symptomatic or progressive liver disease or hepatic failure; cardiac failure, paralytic ileus."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Agranulocytosis, orthostatic hypotension, heart failure, hyperglycemia may develop. Baseline total and differential white blood cell count is required before initiation, during and after termination of treatment. May impair ability to drive or operate machinery. Pregnancy and lactation.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",

          },

          },

          },

  {
    "id": 1211,
    "detail": {
      "success": true,
      "data": {
        "id": 1211,
        "brand_name": "Zithrocin",
        "brand_suffix": null,
        "brand_shot": {
          "original": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/sDsZiJo44heheZdzWJjvwafyQo8xSNxjMDBbQFChZ0k5OAaEWG.jpg",
          "medium": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/sDsZiJo44heheZdzWJjvwafyQo8xSNxjMDBbQFChZ0k5OAaEWG.jpg",
          "small": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/w664t0oxzVfzwQfmOD6jEeT3P2xbMTpDrD72vHXQBuDS1x0I7w.jpg"
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Azithromycin dihydrate",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Macrolides"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Treatment of mild to moderate infections caused by susceptible organisms e.g. respiratory tract, uncomplicated skin and soft tissue, and uncomplicated genital chlamydial infections; nongonococcal urethritis, chancroid and pelvic inflammatory disease."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "500 mg daily for 3 days. <b>Uncomplicated STD caused by <i>Chlamydia trachomatis</i></b>: 1 g as single dose. <b>Uncomplicated gonorrhea</b>: 2 g as single dose. Prophylaxis of disseminated <i>Mycobacterium avium</i>-complex infections: 1.2 g once weekly. <b>For treatment or secondary prophylaxis</b>: 500 mg once daily. <b>Mild to moderate typhoid</b>: 500 mg once daily for 7 days."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity. Severe hepatic impairment."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Renal impairment. Pregnancy and lactation. <b><a href=\"#\" id=\"has_preg_risk\">Pregnancy Risk</a>:</b> Category B.",
            "pregnancy_risk": {
              "header": "Pregnancy Risk Category by the US FDA:",
              "categories": [
                {
                  "title": "A",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in humans do not show any evidence that the product poses a risk to the baby in the womb."
                },
                {
                  "title": "B",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in animals do not show a risk to the baby, but there are not enough compelling studies in humans. Otherwise, animal studies show a risk but studies on humans do not show any."
                },
                {
                  "title": "C",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in animals show a risk to the baby, but there are not enough compelling studies in humans. However, the product may be used in pregnant women in certain situations despite potential risks."
                },
                {
                  "title": "D",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in humans show a risk to the baby. However, the product may be used in pregnant women in certain situations despite the potential risks."
                },
                {
                  "title": "X",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in animals or humans show a risk to the baby in the womb. Also, use or experience in humans shows a risk. The danger of using the product in pregnant women clearly outweighs its benefits."
                }
              ]
            }
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Anorexia, nausea, abdominal discomfort, flatulence, vomiting and diarrhea. Pruritus, urticaria, skin rashes."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "Antacids containing aluminum and magnesium salts; nelfinavir, zidovudine, ergot derivatives, coumarin, cyclosporine, digoxin. Astemizole, cisapride, terfenadine."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Tablet 500 mg (P92.97)."
          }
        ],

  {
    "id": 5,
    "detail": {
      "success": true,
      "data": {
        "id": 5,
        "brand_name": "Zithromax",
        "brand_suffix": null,
        "brand_shot": {
          "original": null,
          "medium": null,
          "small": null
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Azithromycin dihydrate",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Macrolides"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Infections caused by susceptible organisms: lower respiratory tract infections e.g. bronchitis and pneumonia; skin and soft tissue infections, acute otitis media, upper respiratory tract infections e.g. sinusitis and pharyngitis/tonsillitis. Treatment of sexually transmitted disease (STD) due to <i>Chlamydia trachomatis</i> and chancroid due to <i>Haemophilus ducreyi</i> and uncomplicated genital infections due to non-multidrug-resistant <i>Neisseria gonorrhoeae</i>. Prophylaxis against <i>Mycobacterium avium</i>-intracellulare complex (MAC) infection either alone or in combination with rifabutin, and treatment of disseminated MAC (DMAC) in combination with ethambutol in patients with advanced HIV. Treatment of community-acquired pneumonia (CAP) caused by <i>Legionella pneumophila</i> and pelvic inflammatory disease (PID) caused by <i>C. trachomatis</i>, <i>N. gonorrhea</i>, <i>Mycoplasma hominis</i> in patients who require initial IV therapy."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Adults - Treatment of STDs caused by <i>C. trachomatis</i> and <i>H. ducreyi</i>:</b> 1,000 mg single dose. For susceptible <i>N. gonorrhoeae:</i> 1,000 mg or 2,000 mg in combination with 250 mg or 500 mg ceftriaxone according to local clinical treatment guidelines. <b>Prophylaxis against MAC infections in patients with HIV:</b> 1,200 mg once per week. <b>DMAC infections in patients with advanced HIV:</b> 600 mg once a day. <b>CAP:</b> 500 mg IV single daily dose for 2 days, followed by 500 mg oral single daily dose for 7- to 10-day therapy. <b>PID:</b> 500 mg IV single dose for 1-2 days, followed by 250 mg oral single daily dose to complete 7-day therapy. <b>Other infections:</b> 500 mg single daily dose for 3 days (total dosage of 1,500 mg); can be given over 5 days with 500 mg given on day 1, then 250 mg daily on days 2 -5. IV infusion concentration and rate of either 1 mg/mL over 3 hrs, or 2 mg/mL over 1 hr. A 500 mg IV dose should be infused for minimum 1 hr duration. <b>Childn - Any treatment:</b> Total dose of 30 mg/kg, given as single daily dose of 10 mg/kg for 3 days, or given over 5 days with single dose of 10 mg/kg on day 1, then 5 mg/kg on days 2-5. Max total dose: 1,500 mg. Acute otitis media: 30 mg/kg as single dose. <b>Pediatric <i>Streptococcal pharyngitis</i>:</b> Single dose of 10 mg/kg or 20 mg/kg for 3 days. Max 500 mg/day. <b>Susp - 3-day regimen</b> - Childn >45 kg: Adult dose; 36-45 kg: 10 mL (400 mg); 26-35 kg: 7.5 mL (300 mg); 15-25 kg: 5 mL (200 mg); < 15 kg: 10 mg/kg. All doses given once daily for 3 days. <b>5-day regimen</b> - Childn >45 kg: Adult dose; 36-45 kg: 10 mL (400 mg) on day 1, then 5 mL (200 mg) once daily on days 2-5; 26-35 kg: 7.5 mL (300 mg) on day 1, then 3.75 mL (150 mg) once daily on days 2-5; 15-25 kg: 5 mL (200 mg) on day 1, then 2.5 mL (100 mg) once daily on days 2-5; < 15 kg: 10 mg/kg on day 1, then 5 mg/kg once daily on days 2-5."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity to azithromycin, erythromycin, any macrolide or ketolide antibiotic. Coadministration with ergot derivatives."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Discontinue if allergic reaction, signs and symptoms of hepatitis occurs. Patients with significant hepatic disease, severe renal impairment (GFR < 10 mL/min), and diabetes; fructose intolerance, glucose-galactose malabsorption or saccharase-isomaltase deficiency. Infantile hypertrophic pyloric stenosis (IHPS). Observe for signs of superinfection. Overgrowth of <i>C. difficile</i> including <i>C. difficile</i>-associated diarrhea. Patients with congenital or documented QT prolongation, electrolyte disturbance (e.g. hypokalemia and hypomagnesemia), bradycardia, cardiac arrhythmia or cardiac insufficiency; currently receiving drugs that prolong QT interval (e.g. Class IA and III antiarrhythmics, antipsychotics, antidepressants, fluoroquinolones). Should be reconstituted and diluted as directed and administered as IV infusion over not < 60 mins. Do not administer as IV bolus or IM injection. Elderly. Pregnancy and lactation.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Transient episode of mild neutropenia; hearing impairment; nausea, vomiting, diarrhea, loose stools, abdominal discomfort and flatulence; abnormal liver function; allergic skin reactions including rash and angioedema; local pain and inflammation at site of infusion. Moniliasis, vaginitis; thrombocytopenia; anorexia; aggressive reaction, nervousness, agitation, anxiety; dizziness, convulsions, headache, hyperactivity, hypoesthesia, paresthesia, somnolenece, syncope; deafness, tinnitus, vertigo; palpitations, arrhythmias; hypotension; dyspepsia, constipation, pseudomembranous colitis, pancreatitis; hepatitis and cholestatic jaundice; allergic reactions; arthralgia; interstitial nephritis, acute renal failure; asthenia, fatigue, malaise."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "Antacids, cetirizine, didanosine (dideoxyinosine), digoxin and colchicine, ergot, zidovudine, atorvastatin, carbamazepine, cimetidine, coumarin-type oral anticoagulants, ciclosporin, efavirenz, fluconazole, indinavir, methylprednisolone, midazolam, nelfinavir, rifabutin, sildenafil, terfenadine, theophylline, triazolam, trimethoprim/sulfamethoxazole."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Film-coated tablet 250 mg, 500 mg. Powder for oral suspension 200 mg/5 mL x 15 mL, 22.5 mL. Powder for IV infusion 500 mg."
          }
        ],
        "company": {
          "id": 221,
          "name": "Zuellig Pharma Corp."
        },
        "class": "human",
        "form_class": "3",
        "prescription_class": "Rx",
        "description": null,
        "directions": null,
        "instrument": null,
        "sensitivity": null,
        "uses": null,
        "withdrawal": null,
        "dangerous": 0,
        "storage": null,

        "generic_content": "Azithromycin",

          408
        ],

        "is_prescribable": true,
        "therapeutic_class": "Macrolides",
        "trusted_med_id": null,

      }
    }

        "brand_shot": {
          "original": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/BROg2RcugiFGSIExQbonxscL8moZncEjtDOPYAjQUo6avxJmpF.jpg",
          "medium": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/4ZUT3oUS4HfzA5to1w2BaShNk6MIxuwjjKnwznJLCKMCv3L1zI.jpg",
          "small": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/5AbS75EpDQGBIo47DbknSYMLUdPsuJFdWVp31rKcyYLwJKssVc.jpg"
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Ambroxol HCl",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Expectorants (Mucolytics/Mucokinetics/Protussive)"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Non-Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Relief of cough caused by acute and chronic disorders of the respiratory tract with pathologically increased mucus viscosity, e.g. acute and chronic bronchitis, asthmatic bronchitis, and bronchial asthma with disturbances of secretion, elimination, bronchiectasis and to encourage elimination of phlegm during inflammation of the nasal cavity and throat."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Tab</b> - Adults and childn &gt;12 yrs: 1 tab thrice daily for the 1<sup>st</sup> 2-3 days, then 1 tab twice daily or &frac12; tab thrice daily; Childn 6-12 yrs: &frac12; tab 2-3x daily. <b>Sustained-release cap</b> - Adults and childn &gt;12 yrs: 1 cap once daily after meal with plenty of liquid. <b>Double strength syr (30 mg/5 mL)</b> - Adults and childn &gt;12 yrs: 5 mL thrice daily for the 1<sup>st</sup> 2-3 days, then 5 mL twice daily or 2.5 mL thrice daily; Childn 6-12 yrs: 2.5 mL 2-3x daily. <b>Syr (15 mg/5 mL)</b> - Adults and childn &gt;12 yrs: 10 mL thrice daily during the 1<sup>st</sup> 2-3 days, then 10 mL twice daily or 5 mL thrice daily; 6-12 yrs: 5 mL 2-3x daily; 2-5 yrs: 2.5 mL thrice daily; &lt;2 yrs: 2.5 mL twice daily. <b>Oral drops (6 mg/mL)</b> - Childn 13-24 months: 1.25 mL twice daily; 7-12 months: 1 mL twice daily; &le;6 months: 0.5 mL twice daily."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity. Pregnancy (1<sup>st</sup> trimester)."
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "GI disturbances, transient rise in serum aminotransferase."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "Antitussive (e.g. codeine)."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Tablet 30 mg (P10.43). Sustained-release capsule 75 mg (P28.80). Oral drops 6 mg/mL x 15 mL (P74.85). Syrup 15 mg/5 mL x 60 mL (P84.80), Double strength 30 mg/5 mL x 60 mL (P114.80)."
          }
        ],

  {
    "id": 2243,
    "detail": {
      "success": true,
      "data": {
        "id": 2243,
        "brand_name": "Zocor",
        "brand_suffix": null,
        "brand_shot": {
          "original": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/5xPjmQOfkhbeEshagYHtwon9Z5v6vroHWFjQEYQwwgH4ooePNI.jpg",
          "medium": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/5xPjmQOfkhbeEshagYHtwon9Z5v6vroHWFjQEYQwwgH4ooePNI.jpg",
          "small": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/YTY9bUgmmlBcgoNzJ1riwAEnvNicxJA0i2ELNNniOOze4rI3eN.jpg"
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Simvastatin",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Statins"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Coronary heart disease (CHD). Hyperlipidemia, homozygous familial hypercholesterolemia, mild to moderate hypercholesterolemia. Childn with heterozygous familial hypercholesterolemia."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>CHD</b>: 40 mg/day in the evening. <b>Hyperlipidemia</b>: 20 mg/day in the evening. Patients requiring &gt;45% reduction in LDL-C may be started with 40 mg/day in the evening. <b>Mild to moderate hypercholesterolemia</b>: 10 mg/day starting dose. <b>Homozygous familial hypercholesterolemia</b>: 40 mg/day in the evening. <b>Concomitant therapy with other drugs</b> - Fibrates (other than gemfibrozil) or fenofibrate: Max 10 mg/day; Amiodarone, verapamil, or diltiazem: Max 20 mg/day; Amlodipine: Max 40 mg/day. <b>Patients with severe renal insufficiency</b>: Caution on dosage &gt;10 mg/day. <b>Childn with heterozygous familial hypercholesterolemia</b>: 10 mg once daily in the evening; recommended dosing range is 10-40 mg/day. Max 40 mg/day. The 80 mg dose is only recommended in patients at high risk for cardiovascular complications who have not achieved treatment goals on lower doses and when the benefits are expected to outweigh the potential risks."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity. Active liver disease or unexplained persistent elevations of serum transaminases. Concomitant use of potent CYP3A4 inhibitors, gemfibrozil, ciclosporin or danazol. Pregnancy and lactation."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Discontinue if diffuse myalgias, muscle tenderness, unexplained muscle pain, tenderness or weakness, or markedly elevated creatine kinase levels (&gt;10x ULN) occur or if myopathy is diagnosed or suspected. Patients who had complicated medical histories including renal insufficiency or who concomitantly use moderate/potent CYP3A4 inhibitors, fusidic acid, fenofibrate, amiodarone, CCBs, niacin are at high risk of developing myopathy/rhabdomyolysis. Perform liver function tests before starting treatment and periodically thereafter. Alcoholics. History of liver disease. Discontinue if transaminase level rises persistently to 3x ULN. Stop therapy temporarily for a few days prior to elective surgery and when any major medical or surgical conditions supervenes. Childn. <b><a href=\"#\" id=\"has_preg_risk\">Pregnancy Risk</a></b>: Category X.",
            "pregnancy_risk": {
              "header": "Pregnancy Risk Category by the US FDA:",
              "categories": [
                {
                  "title": "A",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in humans do not show any evidence that the product poses a risk to the baby in the womb."
                },
                {
                  "title": "B",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in animals do not show a risk to the baby, but there are not enough compelling studies in humans. Otherwise, animal studies show a risk but studies on humans do not show any."
                },
                {
                  "title": "C",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in animals show a risk to the baby, but there are not enough compelling studies in humans. However, the product may be used in pregnant women in certain situations despite potential risks."
                },
                {
                  "title": "D",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in humans show a risk to the baby. However, the product may be used in pregnant women in certain situations despite the potential risks."
                },
                {
                  "title": "X",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in animals or humans show a risk to the baby in the womb. Also, use or experience in humans shows a risk. The danger of using the product in pregnant women clearly outweighs its benefits."
                }
              ]
            }
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Abdominal pain, constipation, and flatulence. Asthenia and headache. Nausea, diarrhea, rash, dyspepsia, pruritus, alopecia, dizziness, muscle cramps, myalgia, pancreatitis, paresthesia, peripheral neuropathy, vomiting and anemia."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "CYP3A4 inhibitors (e.g. itraconazole, ketoconazole, posaconazole, voriconazole, HIV-protease inhibitors, boceprevir, telaprevir, erythromycin, clarithromycin, telithromycin, nefazodone, and drugs containing cobicistat) and/or transport protein (e.g. OATP1B). Gemfibrozil, ciclosporin, danazol, fusidic acid, amiodarone, CCBs, niacin (&gt;1 g/day), colchicine, daptomycin, grapefruit juice, coumarin anticoagulants. Lomitapide, HMG-CoA reductase inhibitors."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Tablet 20 mg (P30.83)."
          }
        ],
        "company": {
          "id": 104,
          "name": "Organon (Philippines) Incorporated"
        },
        "class": "human",
        "form_class": "3",
        "prescription_class": "Rx",

  {

        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Zoledronic acid",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Bisphosphonates"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Hypercalcemia of malignancy. Prevention of skeletal events in patients with advanced bone malignancies. Treatment of Paget’s disease of bone. Osteoporosis in postmenopausal women."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Hypercalcemia of malignancy: </b>Max 4 mg single dose IV infusion over not < 15 mins. <b>Multiple myeloma and metastatic bone lesions of solid tumors - CrCl >60 mL/min:</b> 4 mg IV infusion over not < 15 mins every 3-4 weeks; <b>50-60 mL/min:</b> 3.5 mg;<b>40-49 mL/min: </b>3.3 mg; <b>30-39 mL/min:</b> 3 mg; <b>< 30 mL/min:</b> not recommended. <b>Treatment of Paget’s disease of bone and Osteoporosis in postmenopausal women - CrCl < 35 mL/min: </b>Contraindicated. Administer with 500 mg oral calcium supplement and a multivitamin containing 400 U of vit D daily."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Pregnancy and lactation. Hypocalcemia. Severe renal impairment with CrCl < 35 mL/min (if indicated for prophylaxis or treatment of Paget’s disease of bone and Osteoporosis in postmenopausal women)."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Patients must be adequately supplemented with calcium and vit D. Adequately rehydrate patients with hypercalcemia of malignancy prior to administration. Renal and hepatic impairment. Women of childbearing potential. Jaw osteonecrosis. Perform dental exams with preventive dentistry. Avoid invasive dental procedures. May cause severe and incapacitating bone, joint and/or muscle pain.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Nausea, fatigue, anemia, bone pain, constipation, fever, vomiting, and dyspnea."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "Aminoglycosides, calcitonin, loop diuretics, nephrotoxic drugs, thalidomide."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Lyophilized powder for IV infusion 4 mg."
          }
        ],

        "content": "Zolpidem tartrate",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Benzodiazepine Related Drugs"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx, Exempt Dangerous Drug"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Short-term treatment of insomnia."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Adults &lt;65 yrs:</b> 5 mg (female) and 5 or 10 mg (male) once daily before bedtime with at least 7-8 hrs remaining before planned time of awakening. Max 10 mg once daily. <b>Elderly or debilitated patients:</b> 5 mg once daily. <b>Hepatic impairment:</b> 5 mg once daily. All doses to be given immediately before bedtime. Should not exceed 4 weeks."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity. Obstructive sleep apnea, myasthenia gravis, severe hepatic insufficiency, acute and/or severe respiratory depression. History of drug abuse, alcohol, or other substances. Childn &lt;18 yrs."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Coadministration with other CNS depressants (e.g. benzodiazepines, opioids, TCAs, alcohol). Abnormal thinking and behavioral changes. Evaluate for co-morbid diagnoses. Patients with depression, respiratory impairment (e.g. sleep apnea, myasthenia gravis), history of addiction to or abuse of drugs or alcohol, concomitant psychiatric illness. Psychological dependence. Discontinue when psychiatric and paradoxical reactions occur. Elderly and debilitated patients. May impair ability to drive or operate machines. Pregnancy and lactation. <b><a href=\"#\" id=\"has_preg_risk\">Pregnancy Risk</a>:</b> Category C.",
            "pregnancy_risk": {
              "header": "Pregnancy Risk Category by the US FDA:",
              "categories": [
                {
                  "title": "A",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in humans do not show any evidence that the product poses a risk to the baby in the womb."
                },
                {
                  "title": "B",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in animals do not show a risk to the baby, but there are not enough compelling studies in humans. Otherwise, animal studies show a risk but studies on humans do not show any."
                },
                {
                  "title": "C",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in animals show a risk to the baby, but there are not enough compelling studies in humans. However, the product may be used in pregnant women in certain situations despite potential risks."
                },
                {
                  "title": "D",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in humans show a risk to the baby. However, the product may be used in pregnant women in certain situations despite the potential risks."
                },
                {
                  "title": "X",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in animals or humans show a risk to the baby in the womb. Also, use or experience in humans shows a risk. The danger of using the product in pregnant women clearly outweighs its benefits."
                }
              ]
            }
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Drowsiness, dizziness, nausea, hangover, and GI disturbance."

          "original": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/XXhyT44zbvMID9FNA8gDe3vjRFashcuhtovluKOukz8NlBdSZn.jpg",

          "small": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/WvNN7ZDnYvKByCoUKMEivvtHWGnqnUVC4XkmHqKgb6SA4MlNqu.jpg"
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Zoledronic acid",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Bisphosphonates"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Hypercalcemia of malignancy. Prevention of skeletal events in patients with advanced bone malignancies. Treatment of Paget’s disease of bone. Osteoporosis in postmenopausal women."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Hypercalcemia of malignancy:</b> Max 4 mg single dose IV infusion over not < 15 mins. <b>Multiple myeloma and metastatic bone lesions of solid tumors - CrCl >60 mL/min:</b> 4 mg IV infusion over not < 15 mins every 3-4 weeks; <b>50-60 mL/min:</b> 3.5 mg; <b>40-49 mL/min:</b> 3.3 mg; <b>30-39 mL/min:</b> 3 mg. Administer with 500 mg oral calcium supplement and a multivitamin containing 400 U of vit D daily."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Pregnancy and lactation."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Not for direct injection. Adequately rehydrate patients with hypercalcemia of malignancy prior to administration. Renal and hepatic impairment. Not recommended in patients with bone metastases with severe renal impairment. Jaw osteonecrosis. Perform dental exams with preventive dentistry and maintain good oral hygiene prior to treatment. Avoid invasive dental procedures. May cause severe and incapacitating bone, joint and/or muscle pain. Patients with asthma. Patients must be adequately supplemented with calcium and vit D. Do not mix with calcium-containing infusion soln. Childn. Women of childbearing potential.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Nausea, fatigue, anemia, bone pain, constipation, fever, vomiting, and dyspnea."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "Aminoglycosides, loop diuretics, nephrotoxic drugs, thalidomide. Hypocalcemia, hypophosphatemia, hypomagnesemia."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Lyophilized powder for IV infusion 4 mg."
          }
        ],

    "id": 4775,
    "detail": {
      "success": true,
      "data": {
        "id": 4775,
        "brand_name": "Zolevi",
        "brand_suffix": null,
        "brand_shot": {
          "original": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/SZAaOOH7Q3wEMhACddH2D0ZMxHB2n9DzAKdvM2oKjTvJW9lJUD.jpg",
          "medium": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/g70tU0EUfAUiW5gw3lMt5Opt3I0IIbD0kEjELkOUlXQA51WIhO.jpg",
          "small": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/YoL5ILAkhr48eIj3yO0GAPlL0P1vUGddMJHe5qV92KhRQvthIq.jpg"
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Levetiracetam",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Pyrrolidine"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Monotherapy in treatment of partial onset seizure, with or without secondary generalization in adults &ge;16 yrs with newly diagnosed epilepsy. Adjunctive therapy in the treatment of partial onset seizure, with or without secondary generalization in adults, adolescents and childn &ge;1 month with epilepsy; myoclonic seizures in adults and adolescents &ge;12 yrs with juvenile myoclonic epilepsy; primary generalized tonic-clonic seizures in adults and childn &gt;12 yrs with idiopathic generalized epilepsy."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "Monotherapy – <b>Adults &ge;16 yrs:</b> 250 mg twice, increase to 500 mg twice daily after 2 weeks. Dose can be further increased by 250 mg twice daily every 2 weeks. Max: 1,500 mg twice daily, increase up to 1,500 mg twice daily. <b>Add-on therapy – Adult &ge;18 yrs and adolescents 12-17 yrs weighing &ge;50 kg:</b> 500 mg twice daily, increase up to 1,500 mg twice daily. Adolescents &lt;50 kg, children, and infants &ge;6 months: 10 mg/kg twice daily. Max dose: 30 mg/kg twice daily. <b>Infants 1&gt; months:</b> 7 mg/kg twice daily. Max: 21 mg/kg twice daily. <b>Renal impairment – Adults and adolescents >50 kg:</b> &ge;80 mL/min/1.73 m<sup>2</sup>: 500-1,500 mg twice daily; 50-79 mL/min/1.73 m<sup>2</sup>: 500-1,000 mg twice daily; 30-49 mL/min/1.73 m<sup>2:</sup> 250-750 mg twice daily; < 30 mL/min/1.73 m<sup>2</sup>: 250-500 mg twice daily; ESRD patients undergoing dialysis: Loading dose 750 mg on 1<sup>st</sup> day, then 500-1,000 mg once daily. 250-500 mg supplemental dose following dialysis. <b>Adolescents &lt;50 kg, childn, and infants 6-23 months:</b>  &ge;80 mL/min/1.73 m<sup>2</sup>: 10-30 mg/kg twice daily; 50-79 mL/min/1.73 m<sup>2</sup>: 10-20 mg/kg twice daily; 30-49 mL/min/1.73 m<sup>2</sup>: 5-15 mg/kg twice daily.; < 30 mL/min/1.73 m<sup>2</sup>: 5-10 mg/kg twice daily; ERSD patients undergoing dialysis: Loading dose 15 mg/kg on the 1st day, then 10-20 mg/kg once daily. 5-10 mg/kg supplemental dose following dialysis. Infants 1-6 months: Infants 1-6 months: &ge;80 mL/min/1.73 m<sup>2</sup>: 7-21 mg/kg twice daily; 50-79 mL/min/1.73 m<sup>2</sup>: 7-14 mg/kg twice daily; 30-49 mL/min/1.73 m<sup>2</sup>: 3.5-10.5 mg/kg twice daily; < 30 mL/min/1.73 m<sup>2</sup>: 3.5-7 mg/kg twice daily; ESRD patients undergoing dialysis: Loading dose 10.5 mg/kg on the 1st day, then 7-14 mg/kg once daily. 3.5-7 mg/kg supplemental dose following dialysis. <b>Hepatic impairment:</b> Reduce daily maintenance dose by 50% when CrCl < 60 mL/min/1.73 m<sup>2</sup>. Taken with or without food."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity to the active substance or other pyrrolidone derivatives."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Renal impairment, decreased blood cell counts, risk of suicidal thoughts. Avoid abrupt withdrawal. Lactation.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Nasopharyngitis, anorexia, depression, hostility/aggression, anxiety, insomnia, nervousness/irritability, somnolence, headache, convulsions, balance disorder, dizziness, lethargy, tremor, vertigo, cough, abdominal pain, diarrhea, dyspepsia, vomiting, nausea, rash, fatigue."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "Methotrexate, oral contraceptives, osmotic laxatives (e.g. macrogol)."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Film-coated tablet 250 mg (P22.00), 500 mg (P30.00), 1g (P62.00)."
          }
        ],
        "company": {
          "id": 93,

        },

  {
    "id": 3085,
    "detail": {
      "success": true,
      "data": {
        "id": 3085,
        "brand_name": "Zoloft",
        "brand_suffix": null,
        "brand_shot": {
          "original": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/tSaUgTq5LzeqzKbg7nYWrAhBtPEUF4fwum0N6HfiG0NUjoIjyr.jpg",
          "medium": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/aoKoUbkRKGVUNfSKlzMcqF1ssZxX898yhNngmmjevNqYWoKtpQ.jpg",
          "small": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/EYddKTyutMJSSEqfALBf9PuMNyx5vxg3rLMbUjpDIOzUk3gZsM.jpg"
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Sertraline HCl",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Selective Serotonin Reuptake Inhibitors (SSRIs)"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Treatment of symptoms of depression, including depression accompanied by symptoms of anxiety, in patients with or without a history of mania; obsessive-compulsive disorder (OCD); panic disorder with or without agoraphobia; post-traumatic stress disorder (PTSD); social phobia (social anxiety disorder); premenstrual dysphoric disorder (PMDD)."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "To be taken once daily, either in the morning or evening. <b>Depression and OCD</b>: Initially 50 mg/day. Max 200 mg/day. <b>Panic disorder, PTSD, and social phobia</b>: Initially 25 mg/day, increased to 50 mg/day after 1 week. <b>OCD</b> - Childn 13-17 yrs: 50 mg/day; 6-12 yrs: 25 mg/day, increased to 50 mg/day after 1 week and further, in increments, if needed up to 200 mg/day. Titration in doses should not occur at intervals of < 1 week. <b>PMDD</b>: Initially 50 mg/day, either daily throughout the menstrual cycle or limited to the luteal phase of the menstrual cycle, may be increased in 50 mg/menstrual cycle increments up to 150 mg/day (menstrual cycle) or 100 mg/day (luteal phase)."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity. Concomitant use of MAOIs or pimozide."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Risk for serotonin syndrome, NMS; activation of mania or hypomania; seizures; sexual dysfunction; bleeding abnormalities; hyponatremia; bone fractures; new-onset DM, loss of glycemic control in patients with or without pre-existing DM. Patients with or at risk for QTc prolongation, Torsades de Pointes; unstable epilepsy; angle-closure glaucoma, hepatic disease. Concomitant use of serotonergic drugs, antipsychotics, and other dopamine agonists; drugs known to affect platelet function (atypical antipsychotics and phenothiazines, TCAs, aspirin, and NSAIDs). May impair ability to drive or operate machinery. Women of childbearing potential. Elderly. Pregnancy and lactation.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Blood and lymphatic; immune; endocrine, metabolism and nutrition; psychiatric; neurologic; eye, ear and labyrinth; cardiovascular; respiratory, thoracic and mediastinal; GI and hepatobiliary; skin and subcutaneous tissue; musculoskeletal and connective tissue; renal and urinary; reproductive and breast disorders."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "Disulfiram, drugs that prolong QTc interval, CNS depressants and alcohol, lithium, phenytoin, sumatriptan, other serotonergic drugs, warfarin. Diazepam, tolbutamide, cimetidine, methimazole. Drugs metabolized by CYP2D6. False-positive urine immunoassay screening for benzodiazepine."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Film-coated tablet 50 mg (P130.80)."
          }
        ],
        "company": {

        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Cefuroxime (Tab: as axetil; IV/IM: as sodium)",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Second Generation Cephalosporins"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Bone and joint infections, bronchitis and other lower respiratory tract infections (RTI), gonorrhea, meningitis, otitis media, peritonitis, pharyngitis, sinusitis, skin including soft tissue infections, urinary tract infections (UTI) and surgical prophylaxis."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Tab - Adults - Uncomplicated UTI</b>: 125 mg twice daily; <b>RTI</b>: 250-500 mg twice daily; <b>Pneumonia or acute exacerbations of chronic bronchitis</b>: IV/IM cefuroxime 1.5 g twice daily or 750 mg twice daily, followed by 500 mg tab twice daily; <b>Lyme disease</b>: 500 mg twice daily for 20 days; <b>Uncomplicated gonorrhea</b>: 1 g single oral dose and 1 g probenecid. <b>IV/IM – Adults</b>: 750 mg to 1.5 g every 8 hrs, usually for 5-10 days. <b>Uncomplicated UTI and pneumonia, skin and skin structure infections, disseminated gonococcal infections</b>: 750 mg every 8 hrs; <b>Severe or complicated infections, bone and joint infections</b>: 1.5 g every 8 hrs; <b>Life-threatening infections or infections due to less susceptible organisms</b>: 1.5 g every 6 hrs. <b>Bacterial meningitis</b>: Not &gt;3 g every 8 hrs; <b>Pre-op prophylaxis</b>: 1.5 g IV pre-op (approx &frac12; to 1 hr before), give 750 mg every 8 hrs if procedure is prolonged. <b>During open heart surgery</b>: 1.5 g IV given at anesthesia induction then every 12 hrs for a total dose 6 g. <b>Childn &gt;3 months</b>: 50-100 mg/kg/day in divided doses every 6-8 hrs; <b>Severe serious infections</b>: 100 mg/kg/day (but not to exceed max adult dose); <b>Bone and joint infections</b>: 150 mg/kg/day in divided doses every 8 hrs; <b>Bacterial meningitis</b>: 200-240 mg/kg/day in divided doses every 6-8 hrs. <b>Renal impairment</b>: Reduce dose."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Porphyria. <b><a href=\"#\" id=\"has_preg_risk\">Pregnancy Risk</a>:</b> Category B.",
            "pregnancy_risk": {
              "header": "Pregnancy Risk Category by the US FDA:",
              "categories": [
                {
                  "title": "A",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in humans do not show any evidence that the product poses a risk to the baby in the womb."
                },
                {
                  "title": "B",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in animals do not show a risk to the baby, but there are not enough compelling studies in humans. Otherwise, animal studies show a risk but studies on humans do not show any."
                },
                {
                  "title": "C",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in animals show a risk to the baby, but there are not enough compelling studies in humans. However, the product may be used in pregnant women in certain situations despite potential risks."
                },
                {
                  "title": "D",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in humans show a risk to the baby. However, the product may be used in pregnant women in certain situations despite the potential risks."
                },
                {
                  "title": "X",
                  "content": "Adequate and compelling studies in animals or humans show a risk to the baby in the womb. Also, use or experience in humans shows a risk. The danger of using the product in pregnant women clearly outweighs its benefits."
                }
              ]
            }
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "GI disturbances, erythema multiforme, SJS and TEN, mild to moderate hearing loss in childn."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "<b>Tab</b>: Probenecid, oral contraceptives. <b>IV/IM</b>: Aminoglycoside antibiotics, metronidazole. False positive in Benedict’s or Fehling’s solutions, Clinitest. False negative with ferricyanide test."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Film-coated tablet 500 mg (P61.26). Powder for injection (IV/IM) 750 mg (P289.60)."
          }
        ],
        "company": {
          "id": 93,
          "name": "Natrapharm, Inc."
        },
        "class": "human",
        "form_class": "3",

  {
    "id": 4514,
    "detail": {
      "success": true,
      "data": {
        "id": 4514,
        "brand_name": "Zondrax",
        "brand_suffix": null,
        "brand_shot": {
          "original": null,
          "medium": null,
          "small": null
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Zoledronic acid",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Bisphosphonates"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Prevention of skeletal-related events in adults with advanced bone malignancies. Treatment of tumor-induced hypercalcemia (TIH)."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "Administered as single IV infusion for not &lt;15 mins. <b>Prevention of skeletal-related events with advanced bone malignancies:</b> 4 mg every 3-4 weeks. Also administer 500 mg oral calcium supplement and 400 IU vitamin D daily. Patients with renal impairment - CrCl >60 mL/min: 4 mg; 50-60 mL/min: 3.5 mg; 40-49 mL/min: 3.3 mg; 30-39 mL/min: 3 mg. <b>Treatment of TIH:</b> 4 mg single dose."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity. Lactation."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Assess patient hydration before therapy. Avoid overhydration in patients at risk of cardiac failure. Monitor calcium, phosphate, and magnesium serum levels. Patients with renal and hepatic impairment, unhealed open soft tissue lesions in mouth. Risk of osteonecrosis in patients with cancer, co-morbid conditions (e.g. anemia, coagulopathies, infection), smoking; history of dental disease, poor oral hygiene, periodontal disease, invasive dental procedures, poorly fitting dentures; concomitant use with chemotherapy, angiogenesis inhibitors, radiotherapy to neck and head, corticosteroids. Do not mix with calcium or other divalent cation-containing infusion solutions e.g. Lactated Ringer’s soln. Discontinue when atypical femur fracture develops. Coadministration with other medicinal products known to cause hypercalcemia. May impair ability to drive or operate machinery. Pregnancy.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Hypophosphatemia, anemia, headache, conjunctivitis, nausea, vomiting, decreased appetite, bone pain, myalgia, arthralgia, generalized pain, renal impairment, fever, flu-like syndrome, increased blood creatinine and urea, hypocalcemia."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "Aminoglycosides, calcitonin or loop diuretics, anti-angiogenic products. Lactated Ringer’s soln."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Concentrated solution for IV infusion 800 mcg/mL x 5 mL (P8,000.00)."
          }
        ],
        "company": {
          "id": 214,
          "name": "The Cathay Drug Co., Inc."
        },
        "class": "human",

  {
    "id": 3192,
    "detail": {
      "success": true,
      "data": {
        "id": 3192,
        "brand_name": "Zonegran",
        "brand_suffix": null,
        "brand_shot": {
          "original": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/NJLqAHDwXaJFxNqUUU0E3RIkTJwnGSH3C0p69OtO25H7iRHA4D.jpg",
          "medium": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/NJLqAHDwXaJFxNqUUU0E3RIkTJwnGSH3C0p69OtO25H7iRHA4D.jpg",
          "small": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/EDFYkAOnFbRQJBS58nalIc05HxJmvA2dQLgceDFi1zTcjBSj6K.jpg"
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Zonisamide",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Benzisoxazoles"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Treatment of epilepsy seizure - Partial seizures: simple partial seizures (focal seizure e.g. Jacksonian type; autonomic seizure, psychomotor seizure); complex partial seizures (psychomotor seizure, focal seizure); secondary generalized tonic-clonic seizure (grand mal). Generalized seizures: tonic-clonic seizures (generalized convulsion, grand mal); tonic seizures (generalized convulsion); atypical absence (petit mal variant). Combined seizures."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Adults</b>: Initially 1-2 tabs/day in 1-3 divided doses, may be gradually increased, up to 2-4 tabs daily in 1-3 divided doses every 1-2 weeks. Max 600 mg/day. <b>Childn &ge;6 yrs</b>: Initially 2-4 mg/kg/day in 1-3 divided doses, may be gradually increased, up to 4-8 mg/kg/day in 1-3 divided doses every 1-2 weeks. Max 12 mg/kg/day."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity to zonisamide or sulfonamides."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Avoid radical reduction of the dose or abrupt discontinuance of the administration. Risk for increased body temp. Patients with preexisting or at risk for severe hepatic dysfunction; renal impairment; nephrolithiasis; hypercalciuria; hyperchloremic or non-anion gap metabolic acidosis; pancreatitis; myopathy; hematologic diseases. Patients with history of suicidal ideation and behavior. May impair ability to drive or operate machines. Debilitated patients. Elderly. Childn. Pregnancy and lactation.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Somnolence, dizziness, anorexia, ecchymosis, affect lability, agitation, anxiety, depression, insomnia, irritability, psychotic disorder, ataxia, bradyphrenia, confusion, convulsion, disturbance in attention, memory impairment, nystagmus, paresthesia, speech disorder, tremor, diplopia, abdominal pain, constipation, diarrhea, dyspepsia, nausea, rash, nephrolithiasis, fatigue, influenza-like illness, weight decreased."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "Phenytoin, carbamazepine, sodium valproate."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Film-coated tablet 100 mg (P40.20)."
          }
        ],
        "company": {

          "name": "Hi-Eisai Pharmaceutical, Inc."
        },

  {
    "id": 992,
    "detail": {
      "success": true,
      "data": {
        "id": 992,
        "brand_name": "Zuredel",
        "brand_suffix": null,
        "brand_shot": {
          "original": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/LF7rbE3uoT5RunN8uQ9tnJ7Y6Zphf3nieC1G0eB2O67mi0m7se.jpg",
          "medium": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/6Wu1JB5LHGNhY4B4tPA2E1lWQevwk6HwRqBCTIebLrbCJ6YU27.jpg",
          "small": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/g5L891rMnSpskHDzZRNJ0lyR8UpdaIyhE2r4JTDqCQyWguQASt.jpg"
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Haloperidol",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Butyrophenones"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Management of manifestations of acute and chronic psychosis, including schizophrenia and manic states. Management of severe behavior problems in childn who have combative, explosive hyperexcitability and in symptomatic control of Gilles de la Tourette’s syndrome."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Adults</b> - <b>Psychoses and associated behavior disorders:</b> 0.5-5 mg 2-3x daily; <b>Severe psychoses or resistant patients:</b> Up to 100 mg daily may be required. Maintenance: 3-10 mg daily. <b>Childn:</b> 0.025-0.050 mg/kg/day in 2 divided doses, increase cautiously as needed."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity. Severe depressive or comatose states, Parkinson's disease, or preexisting Parkinson-like symptoms."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity to other neuroleptic drugs. Angina-prone patients, moderate hypotension, epilepsy, severe impairment of liver or kidney function. Pregnancy and lactation. <b><a href=\"#\" id=\"has_preg_risk\">Pregnancy Risk</a>:</b> Category C.",

        "brand_shot": {
          "original": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/ySmJqNBrfkLOkmXEQ8g9r8anFjhOScWsgpm1OZhCyb14GD8H8L.jpg",
          "medium": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/SQ4tXrOzUyxpujlDUYtOWt26ohpBKFMJBUyXwY9CIPSbW6ctcj.jpg",
          "small": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/aOPO3tXNZaeTvzMzgBV4BppbJ5fxtSMyulR3CjISOR4SdZo3CJ.jpg"
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Spironolactone",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Aldosterone Antagonists and Potassium-Sparing Diuretics"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Management of primary hyperaldosteronism, and edematous conditions for patients with congestive heart failure (CHF), liver cirrhosis accompanied by edema and/or ascites, nephrotic syndrome, essential hypertension (HTN) and hypokalemia."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Primary hyperaldosteronism</b> - Long test: 400 mg daily for 3-4 weeks; Short test: 400 mg daily for 4 days. After diagnosis is established by definitive testing, doses of 100-400 mg daily may be administered in preparation for surgery. In patients unsuitable for surgery, spironolactone may be employed for long-term maintenance therapy. <b>Edema in adults (CHF, liver cirrhosis, nephrotic syndrome)</b>: Initially 100 mg either single or divided doses, but dose may range from 25-200 mg daily. <b>Essential HTN</b>: Initially 50-100 mg daily either single or divided doses. <b>Diuretic-induced hypokalemia</b>: 25-100 mg daily."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity. Patients with anuria, acute renal insufficiency, renal impairment, or hyperkalemia."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Patients with impaired hepatic or renal function. May induce hyperkalemia, dilutional hyponatremia, mild acidosis, gynecomastia. Concomitant use with potassium supplementation (medication or diet), ACE inhibitors, indomethacin, lithium. Monitor for fluid or electrolyte imbalances. Discontinue if hyperkalemia is present. Child. Pregnancy and lactation.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Gastric bleeding, ulceration, gastritis, diarrhea and cramping, nausea, vomiting, gynecomastia, inability to maintain or achieve erection, irregular menses or amenorrhea, postmenopausal bleeding, agranulocytosis, fever, urticaria, maculopapular or erythematous cutaneous eruptions, anaphylactic reactions, vasculitis, mental confusion, ataxia, headache, drowsiness, lethargy; renal dysfunction including renal failure."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "ACE inhibitors, alcohol, barbiturates, narcotics, corticosteroids, pressor amines (e.g. norepinephrine), nondepolorizing skeletal muscle relaxants (e.g. tubocurarine), lithium, NSAIDs, digoxin."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Tablet 25 mg (P15.00), 50 mg (P22.50)."
          }
        ],

          },

            "content": "Bronchial asthma. Croup (viral laryngotracheobronchitis or laryngitis subglottica) in infants and childn."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "Individualized dosage. Recommended air flow rate of 6-8 L/min. Usual fill volume: 2-4 mL. <b>Childn &lt;12 yrs</b>: Max 2 mg/day, should only be considered in severe asthma and during limited periods."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Not for the rapid relief of acute episodes of asthma. Patients with active or quiescent pulmonary TB, fungal or viral infections in the airways, reduced liver function, COPD. Concomitant use with CYP3A4 inhibitors e.g. itraconazole, ketoconazole, HIV protease inhibitors, and cobicistat-containing products. Prolonged or high-dose treatment. Non-steroid dependent patients may be given additional oral corticosteroid regimen for about 2 weeks. Steroid-dependent patients should be in a relatively stable phase when shifting from oral corticosteroid to budesonide. Risk of pneumonia and growth suppression (in childn). Childn. Pregnancy.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "CYP3A4 inhibitors e.g. itraconazole, ketoconazole, HIV protease inhibitors, cobicistat-containing products; estrogens and contraceptive steroids; ACTH stimulation test."

          "small": "https://d1c0x5rkl7k63i.cloudfront.net/upload/drug/brand/image/TpoZLgpQMIlxM8D33KDwyQ0jjDxsKASJQTLRZR7uwhjvbZd8DU.jpg"
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Montelukast sodium, levocetirizine diHCl",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Leukotriene Receptor Antagonists (LTRA)"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Relief of symptoms associated with seasonal and perennial allergic rhinitis."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "Childn 6-14 yrs: 1 tab once daily."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity. Severe renal impairment (CrCl &lt;10 mL/min). Patients with rare hereditary problems of galactose intolerance, the Lapp lactase deficiency or glucose-galactose malabsorption."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Patients with predisposing factors for urinary retention (e.g. spinal cord lesion, prostatic hyperplasia), epilepsy or at risk of convulsion. Renal and hepatic impairment. Concurrent use of alcohol.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "URTI, diarrhea, nausea, vomiting, increased ALT and AST, rash, pyrexia."
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "<b>Montelukast:</b> Phenobarbital, rifampin. <b>Levocetirizine:</b> Theophylline, alcohol, CNS depressants."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Chewable tablet 5 mg/5 mg (P22.68)."
          }
        ],

  {
    "id": 4533,
    "detail": {
      "success": true,
      "data": {
        "id": 4533,
        "brand_name": "Zylam",
        "brand_suffix": null,
        "brand_shot": {
          "original": null,
          "medium": null,
          "small": null
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Midazolam HCl",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Benzodiazepines"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx, Dangerous Drugs"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "For conscious sedation before and during diagnostic or therapeutic procedures with or without local anesthesia, premedication before induction of anesthesia, and sedation in ICU (in adults and childn). Induction of anesthesia, and as sedative component in combined anesthesia (in adults)."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Conscious sedation</b> - &lt;60 yrs: Initially 2-2.5 mg IV administered for 5-10 mins before beginning the procedure, may increase by 1 mg; Elderly &ge;60 yrs, debilitated or chronically ill: Initially 0.5-1 mg IV, may increase by 0.5-1 mg. Administered by slow IV injection at approx rate of 1 mg in 30 secs. Childn 6-12 yrs: Initially 0.025-0.05 mg/kg IV administered over 2-3 mins and wait additional 2-5 mins to fully evaluate sedative effect. Max 10 mg; 6 months-5 yrs: Initially 0.05-0.1 mg/kg IV. Max 6 mg; &gt;6 months: 0.3-0.5 mg/kg (rectal); If IM route is necessary, dose in childn 1-15 yrs is 0.05-0.15 mg/kg. <b>Anesthesia pre-medication</b> - Adults &lt;60 yrs: 0.07-0.1 mg/kg IM; Elderly &ge;60 yrs, debilitated or chronically ill: 0.025-0.05 mg/kg IM. Administered 20-60 mins before induction of anesthesia. Childn 1-15 yrs: 0.08-0.2 mg/kg IM; &gt;6 months: 0.3-0.5 mg/kg (rectal), administered 15-30 mins before induction of anesthesia. <b>Induction of anesthesia</b> - Adults &lt;60 yrs: 0.15-0.2 mg/kg IV (0.3-0.35 without pre-medication). Elderly &ge;60 yrs, debilitated or chronically ill: 0.1-0.2 mg/kg IV (0.15-0.3 mg without pre-medication). <b>Sedative component in combined anesthesia</b> - Adults &lt;60 yrs: 0.03-0.1 mg/kg IV (as intermittent small doses or per hr as continuous infusion). Elderly &ge;60 yrs, debilitated or chronically ill: Lower doses. <b>Sedation in ICU</b> - Adults, debilitated or chronically ill patients: Initially 0.03-0.3 mg/kg IV in increments of 1-2.5 mg over 20-30 secs. Maintenance dose: 0.03-0.2 mg/kg/hr; Childn &gt;6 months: Initially 0.05-0.2 mg/kg/hr IV administered over at least 2-3 mins, followed by 0.06-0.12 mg/kg/hr continuous IV infusion; Neonates &gt;32 weeks and childn up to 6 months: 0.06 mg/kg/hr IV; &lt;32 weeks gestational age: 0.03 mg/kg/hr IV."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Do not withdraw abruptly. Avoid rapid IV administration. Patients with chronic respiratory insufficiency, chronic renal failure, impaired liver or heart function, myasthenia gravis; chronically ill or debilitated. History of alcohol or drug abuse. Concomitant use with CY3A4 inhibitors and inducers. Neonates and premature infants. Childn, especially with CV instability. Elderly &gt;60 yrs. Pregnancy and lactation.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Drug Interactions",
            "content": "Itraconazole, fluconazole, ketoconazole, verapamil, diltiazem, erythromycin, clarithromycin. Other CNS depressants, inhalational anesthetics."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Solution for injection (IM/IV/Rectal) 1 mg/mL x 5 mL."
          }
        ],
        "company": {
          "id": 214,
          "name": "The Cathay Drug Co., Inc."
        },
        "class": "human",
        "form_class": "6",

  {

    "detail": {
      "success": true,
      "data": {
        "id": 1110,

          "original": null,

          "small": null
        },
        "brand_status": 1,
        "content": "Mometasone furoate",
        "brand_info": [
          {
            "title": "Therapeutic class",
            "content": "Nasal Decongestants, Antiallergics & Related Drugs"
          },
          {
            "title": "Regulatory Classification",
            "content": "Rx"
          },
          {
            "title": "Indications",
            "content": "Treatment of symptoms of seasonal allergic or perennial rhinitis in adults and childn &ge;6 yrs. Treatment of nasal polyps in adults &ge;18 yrs."
          },
          {
            "title": "Dose",
            "content": "<b>Seasonal or perennial allergic rhinitis - Adults (including elderly) and childn &ge;12 yrs</b>: 2 actuations in each nostril once daily. Maintenance: 1 actuation each nostril. Max: 4 actuations in each nostril once daily. <b>Childn 6-11 yrs</b>: 1 actuation in each nostril once daily. <b>Nasal polyposis</b>: 2 actuations in each nostril once daily. If after 5-6 weeks symptoms are inadequately controlled, may be increased to 2 actuations in each nostril twice daily. Shake well before use."
          },
          {
            "title": "Contraindications",
            "content": "Hypersensitivity. Untreated localized infection involving the nasal mucosa and recent nasal surgery or trauma."
          },
          {
            "title": "Precautions",
            "content": "Patients with active or quiescent tuberculous infection of the respiratory tract, or in untreated fungal, bacterial, systemic viral infections or ocular herpes simplex. Periodic exam for possible changes in the nasal mucosa during long-term use. Discontinue if localized fungal infection of the nose or pharynx develops. Pregnancy and lactation.",
            "pregnancy_risk": null
          },
          {
            "title": "Side Effects",
            "content": "Epistaxis, pharyngitis, nasal burning, nasal irritation and ulceration, headache. URTI, throat irritation."
          },
          {
            "title": "Packaging",
            "content": "Nasal spray (suspension) 50 mcg/actuation x 120 actuations (P515.20)."
          }
        ],
